a new interface to the Cloudflare stack&quot;],&quot;description&quot;:[0,&quot;Agent Lee is an in-dashboard agent that shifts Cloudflareâs interface from manual tab-switching to a single prompt. Using sandboxed TypeScript, it helps you troubleshoot and manage your stack as a grounded technical collaborator.\n&quot;],&quot;imgPreview&quot;:[0,&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/29iboLzcmSDBSB70jCzl30/92685abd0d30671d0ddcae033f9903d8/BLOG-3231_OG.png&quot;]}],&quot;publicly_index&quot;:[0,true]}],[0,{&quot;id&quot;:[0,&quot;3r2ykMs0LTSPwVHmVWldCy&quot;],&quot;title&quot;:[0,&quot;Project Think: building the next generation of AI agents on Cloudflare&quot;],&quot;slug&quot;:[0,&quot;project-think&quot;],&quot;excerpt&quot;:[0,&quot;Announcing a preview of the next edition of the Agents SDK â from lightweight primitives to a batteries-included platform for AI agents that think, act, and persist.\n&quot;],&quot;featured&quot;:[0,true],&quot;html&quot;:[0,&quot;&lt;p&gt;Today, we&amp;#39;re introducing Project Think: the next generation of the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Agents SDK&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. Project Think is a set of new primitives for building long-running agents (durable execution, sub-agents, sandboxed code execution, persistent sessions) and an opinionated base class that wires them all together. Use the primitives to build exactly what you need, or use the base class to get started fast.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Something happened earlier this year that changed how we think about AI. Tools like &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/badlogic/pi-mono\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Pi&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/openclaw\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;OpenClaw&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/agents\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Claude Code&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, and &lt;a href=\&quot;https://openai.com/codex\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Codex&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; proved a simple but powerful idea: give an LLM the ability to read files, write code, execute it, and remember what it learned, and you get something that looks less like a developer tool and more like a general-purpose assistant.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;These coding agents aren&amp;#39;t just writing code anymore. People are using them to manage calendars, analyze datasets, negotiate purchases, file taxes, and automate entire business workflows. The pattern is always the same: the agent reads context, reasons about it, writes code to take action, observes the result, and iterates. Code is the universal medium of action.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Our team has been using these coding agents every day. And we kept running into the same walls:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;They only run on your laptop or an expensive VPS:&lt;/b&gt; there&amp;#39;s no sharing, no collaboration, no handoff between devices.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;They&amp;#39;re expensive when idle&lt;/b&gt;: a fixed monthly cost whether the agent is working or not. Scale that to a team, or a company, and it adds up fast.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;They require management and manual setup&lt;/b&gt;: installing dependencies, managing updates, configuring identity and secrets.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;p&gt;And there&amp;#39;s a deeper structural issue. Traditional applications serve many users from one instance. As mentioned in our Welcome to Agents Week post, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/welcome-to-agents-week/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;agents are one-to-one&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. Each agent is a unique instance, serving one user, running one task. A restaurant has a menu and a kitchen optimized to churn out dishes at volume. An agent is more like a personal chef: different ingredients, different techniques, different tools every time.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;That fundamentally changes the scaling math. If a hundred million knowledge workers each use an agentic assistant at even modest concurrency, you need capacity for tens of millions of simultaneous sessions. At current per-container costs, that&amp;#39;s unsustainable. We need a different foundation.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;That&amp;#39;s what we&amp;#39;ve been building.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;introducing-project-think\&quot;&gt;Introducing Project Think&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#introducing-project-think\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Project Think ships a set of new primitives for the Agents SDK:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Durable execution&lt;/b&gt; with fibers: crash recovery, checkpointing, automatic keepalive&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Sub-agents&lt;/b&gt;: isolated child agents with their own SQLite and typed RPC&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Persistent sessions&lt;/b&gt;: tree-structured messages, forking, compaction, full-text search&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Sandboxed code execution&lt;/b&gt;: Dynamic Workers, codemode, runtime npm resolution&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;The execution ladder&lt;/b&gt;: workspace, isolate, npm, browser, sandbox&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Self-authored extensions&lt;/b&gt;: agents that write their own tools at runtime&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;p&gt;Each of these is usable directly with the Agent base class. Build exactly what you need with the primitives, or use the Think base class to get started fast. Let&amp;#39;s look at what each one does.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;long-running-agents\&quot;&gt;Long-running agents&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#long-running-agents\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Agents, as they exist today, are ephemeral. They run for a session, tied to a single process or device, and then they are gone. A coding agent that dies when your laptop sleeps, thatâs a tool. An agent that persists â that can wake up on demand, continue work after interruptions, and carry forward the state without depending on your local runtime â that starts to look like infrastructure. And it changes the scaling model for agents completely.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The Agents SDK builds on &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Durable Objects&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to give every agent an identity, persistent state, and the ability to wake on message. This is the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Actor_model\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;actor model&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;: each agent is an addressable entity with its own SQLite database. It consumes zero compute when hibernated. When something happens (an HTTP request, a WebSocket message, a scheduled alarm, an inbound email) the platform wakes the agent, loads its state, and hands it the event. The agent does its work, then goes back to sleep.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;&lt;p&gt;\n&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;VMs / Containers&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Durable Objects&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Idle cost&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Full compute cost, always&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Zero (hibernated)&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Scaling&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Provision and manage capacity&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Automatic, per-agent&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;State&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;External database required&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Built-in SQLite&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Recovery&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;You build it (process managers, health checks)&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Platform restarts, state survives&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Identity / routing&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;You build it (load balancers, sticky sessions)&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Built-in (name â agent)&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;10,000 agents, each active 1% of the time&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;10,000 always-on instances&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;~100 active at any moment&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;p&gt;This changes the economics of running agents at scale. Instead of &amp;quot;one expensive agent per power user,&amp;quot; you can build &amp;quot;one agent per customer&amp;quot; or &amp;quot;one agent per task&amp;quot; or &amp;quot;one agent per email thread.&amp;quot; The marginal cost of spawning a new agent is effectively zero.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;surviving-crashes-durable-execution-with-fibers\&quot;&gt;Surviving crashes: durable execution with fibers&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#surviving-crashes-durable-execution-with-fibers\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;An LLM call takes 30 seconds. A multi-turn agent loop can run for much longer. At any point during that window, the execution environment can vanish: a deploy, a platform restart, hitting resource limits. The upstream connection to the model provider is severed permanently, in-memory state is lost, and connected clients see the stream stop with no explanation.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/api-reference/durable-execution/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;runFiber()&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt; solves this. A fiber is a durable function invocation: registered in SQLite before execution begins, checkpointable at any point via &lt;code&gt;stash()&lt;/code&gt;, and recoverable on restart via &lt;code&gt;onFiberRecovered&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;import { Agent } from &amp;quot;agents&amp;quot;;\n\nexport class ResearchAgent extends Agent {\n  async startResearch(topic: string) {\n    void this.runFiber(&amp;quot;research&amp;quot;, async (ctx) =&amp;gt; {\n      const findings = [];\n\n      for (let i = 0; i &amp;lt; 10; i++) {\n        const result = await this.callLLM(`Research step ${i}: ${topic}`);\n        findings.push(result);\n\n        // Checkpoint: if evicted, we resume from here\n        ctx.stash({ findings, step: i, topic });\n\n        this.broadcast({ type: &amp;quot;progress&amp;quot;, step: i });\n      }\n\n      return { findings };\n    });\n  }\n\n  async onFiberRecovered(ctx) {\n    if (ctx.name === &amp;quot;research&amp;quot; &amp;amp;&amp;amp; ctx.snapshot) {\n      const { topic } = ctx.snapshot;\n      await this.startResearch(topic);\n    }\n  }\n}\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;The SDK keeps the agent alive automatically during fiber execution, no special configuration needed. For work measured in minutes, keepAlive() / keepAliveWhile() prevents eviction during active work. For longer operations (CI pipelines, design reviews, video generation) the agent starts the work, persists the job ID, hibernates, and wakes on callback.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;delegating-work-sub-agents-via-facets\&quot;&gt;Delegating work: sub-agents via Facets&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#delegating-work-sub-agents-via-facets\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;A single agent shouldn&amp;#39;t do everything itself. &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/api-reference/sub-agents/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Sub-agents&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; are child Durable Objects colocated with the parent via &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/durable-object-facets-dynamic-workers/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Facets&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, each with their own isolated SQLite and execution context:&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;import { Agent } from &amp;quot;agents&amp;quot;;\n\nexport class ResearchAgent extends Agent {\n  async search(query: string) { /* ... */ }\n}\n\nexport class ReviewAgent extends Agent {\n  async analyze(query: string) { /* ... */ }\n}\n\nexport class Orchestrator extends Agent {\n  async handleTask(task: string) {\n    const researcher = await this.subAgent(ResearchAgent, &amp;quot;research&amp;quot;);\n    const reviewer = await this.subAgent(ReviewAgent, &amp;quot;review&amp;quot;);\n\n    const [research, review] = await Promise.all([\n      researcher.search(task),\n      reviewer.analyze(task)\n    ]);\n\n    return this.synthesize(research, review);\n  }\n}\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Sub-agents are isolated at the storage level. Each one gets its own SQLite database, and thereâs no implicit sharing of data between them. This is enforced by the runtime where sub-agent RPC latency is a function call. TypeScript catches misuse at compile time.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;conversations-that-persist-the-session-api\&quot;&gt;Conversations that persist: the Session API&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#conversations-that-persist-the-session-api\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Agents that run for days or weeks need more than the typical flat list of messages. The experimental &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/api-reference/sessions/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Session API&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; models this explicitly. Available on the Agent base class, conversations are stored as trees, where each message has a parent_id. This enables forking (explore an alternative without losing the original path), non-destructive compaction (summarize older messages rather than deleting them), and full-text search across conversation history via &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.sqlite.org/fts5.html\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;FTS5&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;import { Agent } from &amp;quot;agents&amp;quot;;\nimport { Session, SessionManager } from &amp;quot;agents/experimental/memory/session&amp;quot;;\n\nexport class MyAgent extends Agent {\n  sessions = SessionManager.create(this);\n\n  async onStart() {\n    const session = this.sessions.create(&amp;quot;main&amp;quot;);\n    const history = session.getHistory();\n    const forked = this.sessions.fork(session.id, messageId, &amp;quot;alternative-approach&amp;quot;);\n  }\n}\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Session is usable directly with &lt;code&gt;Agent&lt;/code&gt;, and it&amp;#39;s the storage layer that the &lt;code&gt;Think&lt;/code&gt; base class builds on.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;from-tool-calls-to-code-execution\&quot;&gt;From tool calls to code execution&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#from-tool-calls-to-code-execution\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Conventional tool-calling has an awkward shape. The model calls a tool, pulls the result back through the context window, calls another tool, pulls that back, and so on. As the tool surface grows, this gets both expensive and clumsy. A hundred files means a hundred round-trips through the model.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;But &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/code-mode/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;models are better at writing code to use a system than they are at playing the tool-calling game&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. This is the insight behind &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/tree/main/packages/codemode\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/codemode&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;: instead of sequential tool calls, the LLM writes a single program that handles the entire task.&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;// The LLM writes this. It runs in a sandboxed Dynamic Worker.\nconst files = await tools.find({ pattern: &amp;quot;**/*.ts&amp;quot; });\nconst results = [];\nfor (const file of files) {\n  const content = await tools.read({ path: file });\n  if (content.includes(&amp;quot;TODO&amp;quot;)) {\n    results.push({ file, todos: content.match(/\\/\\/ TODO:.*/g) });\n  }\n}\nreturn results;\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Instead of 100 round-trips to the model, you just run a single program. This leads to fewer tokens used, faster execution, and better results. The &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/mcp\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cloudflare API MCP server&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; demonstrates this at scale. We expose only two tools &lt;code&gt;(search()&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code&gt;execute())&lt;/code&gt;, which consume ~1,000 tokens, vs. ~1.17 million tokens for the naive tool-per-endpoint equivalent. This is a 99.9% reduction.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;the-missing-primitive-safe-sandboxes\&quot;&gt;The missing primitive: safe sandboxes&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-missing-primitive-safe-sandboxes\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Once you accept that models should write code on behalf of users, the question becomes: where does that code run? Not eventually, not after a product team turns it into a roadmap item. Right now, for this user, against this system, with tightly defined permissions.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/dynamic-workers/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Dynamic Workers&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; are that sandbox. A fresh V8 isolate spun up at runtime, in milliseconds, with a few megabytes of memory. That&amp;#39;s roughly 100x faster and up to 100x more memory-efficient than a container. You can start a new one for every single request, run a snippet of code, and throw it away.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The critical design choice is the capability model. Instead of starting with a general-purpose machine and trying to constrain it, Dynamic Workers begin with almost no ambient authority (&lt;code&gt;globalOutbound: null&lt;/code&gt;, no network access) and the developer grants capabilities explicitly, resource by resource, through bindings. We go from asking &amp;quot;how do we stop this thing from doing too much?&amp;quot; to &amp;quot;what exactly do we want this thing to be able to do?&amp;quot;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This is the right question for agent infrastructure.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;the-execution-ladder\&quot;&gt;The execution ladder&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-execution-ladder\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;This capability model leads naturally to a spectrum of compute environments, an &lt;b&gt;execution ladder&lt;/b&gt; that the agent escalates through as needed:&lt;/p&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/6yokfTVcg8frH4snf7c4sp/2306d721650b4956b28e2198f7cf915d/BLOG-3200_2.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3200 2\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;1288\&quot; height=\&quot;1202\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Tier 0&lt;/b&gt; is the Workspace, a durable virtual filesystem backed by SQLite and R2. Read, write, edit, search, grep, diff. Powered by &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.npmjs.com/package/@cloudflare/shell\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/shell&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Tier 1&lt;/b&gt; is a Dynamic Worker: LLM-generated JavaScript running in a sandboxed isolate with no network access. Powered by &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.npmjs.com/package/@cloudflare/codemode\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/codemode&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Tier 2&lt;/b&gt; adds npm. &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/tree/main/packages/worker-bundler\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/worker-bundler&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt; fetches packages from the registry, bundles them with esbuild, and loads the result into the Dynamic Worker. The agent writes &lt;code&gt;import { z } from &amp;quot;zod&amp;quot;&lt;/code&gt; and it just works.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Tier 3&lt;/b&gt; is a headless browser via &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/browser-rendering/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cloudflare Browser Run&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. Navigate, click, extract, screenshot. Useful when the service doesn&amp;#39;t support agents yet via MCP or APIs.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Tier 4&lt;/b&gt; is a &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/sandbox/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cloudflare Sandbox&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; configured with your toolchains, repos, and dependencies: &lt;code&gt;git clone, npm test, cargo build&lt;/code&gt;, synced bidirectionally with the Workspace.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The key design principle: &lt;b&gt;the agent should be useful at Tier 0 alone, where each tier is additive.&lt;/b&gt; The user can add capabilities as they go.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;building-blocks-not-a-framework\&quot;&gt;Building blocks, not a framework&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#building-blocks-not-a-framework\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;All of these primitives are available as standalone packages. &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/dynamic-workers/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Dynamic Workers&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/tree/main/packages/codemode\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/codemode&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/tree/main/packages/worker-bundler\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/worker-bundler&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, and &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.npmjs.com/package/@cloudflare/shell\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/shell&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt; (a durable filesystem with tools) are all usable directly with the Agent base class. You can combine them to give any agent a workspace, code execution, and runtime package resolution without adopting an opinionated framework.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;the-platform\&quot;&gt;The platform&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-platform\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Here&amp;#39;s the complete stack for building agents on Cloudflare:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Capability&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;What it does&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Powered by&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Per-agent isolation&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Every agent is its own world&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Durable Objects&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; (DOs)&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Zero cost when idle&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;$0 until the agent wakes up&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/best-practices/websockets/#websocket-hibernation-api\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;DO Hibernation&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Persistent state&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Queryable, transactional storage&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/best-practices/access-durable-objects-storage/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;DO SQLite&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Durable filesystem&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Files that survive restarts&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Workspace (SQLite + &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;R2&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;)&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Sandboxed code execution&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Run LLM-generated code safely&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/dynamic-workers/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Dynamic Workers&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; + &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/tree/main/packages/codemode\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/codemode&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Runtime dependencies&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;import * from react&lt;/code&gt; just works&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/tree/main/packages/worker-bundler\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;@cloudflare/worker-bundler&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Web automation&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Browse, navigate, fill forms&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/browser-rendering/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Sandboxes&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Scheduled execution&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Proactive, not just reactive&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/api/alarms/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;DO Alarms + Fibers&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Real-time streaming&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Token-by-token to any client&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;WebSockets&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;External tools&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Connect to any tool server&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;MCP&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Agent coordination&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Typed RPC between agents&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Sub-agents (&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/dynamic-workers/usage/durable-object-facets/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Facets&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;)&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Model access&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Connect to an LLM to power the agent&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/ai-gateway/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;AI Gateway&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; + &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers-ai/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Workers AI&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; (or Bring Your Own Model)&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;p&gt;Each of these is a building block. Together, they form something new: a platform where anyone can build, deploy, and run AI agents as capable as the ones running on your local machine today, but &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/learning/serverless/what-is-serverless/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;serverless&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, durable, and safe by construction. &lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;the-think-base-class\&quot;&gt;The Think base class&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-think-base-class\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Now that you&amp;#39;ve seen the primitives, here&amp;#39;s what happens when you wire them all together.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;Think&lt;/code&gt; is an opinionated harness that handles the full chat lifecycle: agentic loop, message persistence, streaming, tool execution, stream resumption, and extensions. You focus on what makes your agent unique.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The minimal subclass looks like this:&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;import { Think } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/think&amp;quot;;\nimport { createWorkersAI } from &amp;quot;workers-ai-provider&amp;quot;;\n\nexport class MyAgent extends Think&amp;lt;Env&amp;gt; {\n  getModel() {\n    return createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI })(\n      &amp;quot;@cf/moonshotai/kimi-k2.5&amp;quot;\n    );\n  }\n}\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Thatâs effectively all you need to have a working chat agent with streaming, persistence, abort/cancel, error handling, resumable streams, and a built-in workspace filesystem. Deploy with &lt;code&gt;npx wrangler deploy&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Think makes decisions for you. When you need more control, you can override the ones you care about:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Override&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Purpose&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;getModel()&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Return the &lt;code&gt;LanguageModel&lt;/code&gt; to use&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;getSystemPrompt()&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;System prompt&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;getTools()&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;AI SDK compatible &lt;code&gt;ToolSet&lt;/code&gt; for the agentic loop&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;maxSteps&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Max tool-call rounds per turn&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;configureSession()&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;p&gt;Context blocks, compaction, search, skills&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;p&gt;Under the hood, Think runs the complete agentic loop on every turn: it assembles the context (base instructions + tool descriptions + skills + memory + conversation history), calls &lt;code&gt;streamText&lt;/code&gt;, executes tool calls (with output truncation to prevent context blowup), appends results, loops until the model is done or the step limit is reached. All messages are persisted after each turn.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;lifecycle-hooks\&quot;&gt;Lifecycle hooks&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#lifecycle-hooks\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Think gives you hooks at every stage of the chat turn, without requiring you to own the whole pipeline:&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;beforeTurn()\n  â streamText()\n    â beforeToolCall()\n    â afterToolCall()\n  â onStepFinish()\nâ onChatResponse()\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Switch to a lower cost model for follow-up turns, limit the tools it can use, and pass in client-side context on each turn. Also log every tool call to analytics and automatically trigger one more follow-up turn after the model completes, all without replacing &lt;code&gt;onChatMessage&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;persistent-memory-and-long-conversations\&quot;&gt;Persistent memory and long conversations&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#persistent-memory-and-long-conversations\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Think builds on &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/api-reference/sessions/?cf_target_id=E7A3D837FA7DC4C7DDA822B3DE0F831B\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Session API&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; as its storage layer, giving you tree-structured messages with branching built in.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;On top of that, it adds persistent memory through &lt;b&gt;context blocks&lt;/b&gt;. These are structured sections of the system prompt that the model can read and update over time, and they persist across hibernation&lt;b&gt;.&lt;/b&gt;The model sees &amp;quot;MEMORY (Important facts, use set_context to update) [42%, 462/1100 tokens]&amp;quot; and can proactively remember things.&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;configureSession(session: Session) {\n  return session\n    .withContext(&amp;quot;soul&amp;quot;, {\n      provider: { get: async () =&amp;gt; &amp;quot;You are a helpful coding assistant.&amp;quot; }\n    })\n    .withContext(&amp;quot;memory&amp;quot;, {\n      description: &amp;quot;Important facts learned during conversation.&amp;quot;,\n      maxTokens: 2000\n    })\n    .withCachedPrompt();\n}\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Sessions are flexible. You can run multiple conversations per agent and fork them to try a different direction without losing the original.&lt;b&gt;Â &lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;As context grows, Think handles limits with non-destructive compaction. Older messages are summarized instead of removed, while the full history remains stored in SQLite.&lt;b&gt;Â &lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Search is built in as well. Using FTS5, you can query conversation history within a session or across all the sessions. The agent is also able to search its own past using&lt;b&gt; &lt;/b&gt;&lt;code&gt;search_context&lt;/code&gt; tool.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;the-full-execution-ladder-wired-in\&quot;&gt;The full execution ladder, wired in&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-full-execution-ladder-wired-in\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Think integrates the entire execution ladder into a single &lt;code&gt;getTools()&lt;/code&gt; return:&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;import { Think } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/think&amp;quot;;\nimport { createWorkspaceTools } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/think/tools/workspace&amp;quot;;\nimport { createExecuteTool } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/think/tools/execute&amp;quot;;\nimport { createBrowserTools } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/think/tools/browser&amp;quot;;\nimport { createSandboxTools } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/think/tools/sandbox&amp;quot;;\nimport { createExtensionTools } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/think/tools/extensions&amp;quot;;\n\nexport class MyAgent extends Think&amp;lt;Env&amp;gt; {\n  extensionLoader = this.env.LOADER;\n\n  getModel() {\n    /* ... */\n  }\n\n  getTools() {\n    return {\n      execute: createExecuteTool({\n        tools: createWorkspaceTools(this.workspace),\n        loader: this.env.LOADER\n      }),\n      ...createBrowserTools(this.env.BROWSER),\n      ...createSandboxTools(this.env.SANDBOX), // configured per-agent: toolchains, repos, snapshots\n      ...createExtensionTools({ manager: this.extensionManager! }),\n      ...this.extensionManager!.getTools()\n    };\n  }\n}\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            \n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;self-authored-extensions\&quot;&gt;Self-authored extensions&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#self-authored-extensions\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Think takes code execution one step further. An agent can write its own extensions: TypeScript programs that run in Dynamic Workers, declaring permissions for network access and workspace operations.&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-JSON\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-JSON\&quot;&gt;{\n  &amp;quot;name&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;github&amp;quot;,\n  &amp;quot;description&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;GitHub integration: PRs, issues, repos&amp;quot;,\n  &amp;quot;tools&amp;quot;: [&amp;quot;create_pr&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;list_issues&amp;quot;, &amp;quot;review_pr&amp;quot;],\n  &amp;quot;permissions&amp;quot;: {\n    &amp;quot;network&amp;quot;: [&amp;quot;api.github.com&amp;quot;],\n    &amp;quot;workspace&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;read-write&amp;quot;\n  }\n}\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Think&amp;#39;s &lt;code&gt;ExtensionManager&lt;/code&gt; bundles the extension (optionally with npm deps via &lt;code&gt;@cloudflare/worker-bundler&lt;/code&gt;), loads it into a Dynamic Worker, and registers the new tools. The extension persists in DO storage and survives hibernation. The next time the user asks about pull requests, the agent has a &lt;code&gt;github_create_pr &lt;/code&gt;tool that didn&amp;#39;t exist 30 seconds ago.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This is the kind of self-improvement loop that makes agents genuinely more useful over time. Not through fine-tuning or RLHF, but through code. The agent is able to write new capabilities for itself, all in sandboxed, auditable, and revocable TypeScript.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;sub-agent-rpc\&quot;&gt;Sub-agent RPC&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#sub-agent-rpc\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Think also works as a sub-agent, called via &lt;code&gt;chat()&lt;/code&gt; over RPC from a parent, with streaming events via callback:&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;const researcher = await this.subAgent(ResearchSession, &amp;quot;research&amp;quot;);\nconst result = await researcher.chat(`Research this: ${task}`, streamRelay);\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Each child gets its own conversation tree, memory, tools, and model. The parent doesn&amp;#39;t need to know the details.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;getting-started\&quot;&gt;Getting started&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#getting-started\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Project Think is experimental. The API surface is stable but will continue to evolve in the coming days and weeks. We&amp;#39;re already using it internally to build our own background agent infrastructure, and we&amp;#39;re sharing it early so you can build alongside us.&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Shell\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Shell\&quot;&gt;npm install @cloudflare/think agents ai @cloudflare/shell zod workers-ai-provider&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            \n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;// src/server.ts\nimport { Think } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/think&amp;quot;;\nimport { createWorkersAI } from &amp;quot;workers-ai-provider&amp;quot;;\nimport { routeAgentRequest } from &amp;quot;agents&amp;quot;;\n\nexport class MyAgent extends Think&amp;lt;Env&amp;gt; {\n  getModel() {\n    return createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI })(\n      &amp;quot;@cf/moonshotai/kimi-k2.5&amp;quot;\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env) {\n    return (\n      (await routeAgentRequest(request, env)) ||\n      new Response(&amp;quot;Not found&amp;quot;, { status: 404 })\n    );\n  }\n} satisfies ExportedHandler&amp;lt;Env&amp;gt;;\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            \n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-Typescript\&quot;&gt;// src/client.tsx\nimport { useAgent } from &amp;quot;agents/react&amp;quot;;\nimport { useAgentChat } from &amp;quot;@cloudflare/ai-chat/react&amp;quot;;\n\nfunction Chat() {\n  const agent = useAgent({ agent: &amp;quot;MyAgent&amp;quot; });\n  const { messages, sendMessage, status } = useAgentChat({ agent });\n  // Render your chat UI\n}\n&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            &lt;p&gt;Think speaks the same WebSocket protocol as &lt;code&gt;@cloudflare/ai-chat&lt;/code&gt;, so existing UI components work out of the box. If you&amp;#39;ve built on &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/agents/api-reference/chat-agents/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;u&gt;AIChatAgent&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, your client code doesn&amp;#39;t change.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;the-third-wave\&quot;&gt;The third wave&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-third-wave\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;We see three waves of AI agents:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;The first wave was chatbots.&lt;/b&gt; They were stateless, reactive, and fragile. Every conversation started from scratch with no memory, no tools, and no ability to act. This made them useful for answering questions, but limited them to only answering questions.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;The second wave was coding agents.&lt;/b&gt; These are stateful, tool-using and far more capable tools like Pi, Claude Code, OpenClaw, and Codex. These agents can read codebases, write code, execute it, and iterate. These proved that an LLM with the right tools is a general-purpose machine, but they run on your laptop, for one user, with no durability guarantees.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Now we are entering the third wave: agents as infrastructure.&lt;/b&gt; Durable, distributed, structurally safe, and serverless. These are agents that run on the Internet, survive failures, cost nothing when idle, and enforce security through architecture rather than behavior. Agents that any developer can build and deploy for any number of users.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This is the direction weâre betting on.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The Agents SDK is already powering thousands of production agents. With Project Think and the the primitives it introduces, we&amp;#39;re adding the missing pieces to make those agents dramatically more capable: persistent workspaces, sandboxed code execution, durable long-running tasks, structural security, sub-agent coordination, and self-authored extensions.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;It&amp;#39;s available today in preview. We&amp;#39;re building alongside you, and we&amp;#39;d genuinely love to see what you (and your coding agent) create with it.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;hr/&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;Think is part of the Cloudflare Agents SDK, available as @cloudflare/think. The features described in this post are in preview. APIs may change as we incorporate feedback. Check the &lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/blob/main/docs/think/index.md\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;&lt;u&gt;documentation&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt; and &lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/cloudflare/agents/tree/main/examples/assistant\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;&lt;u&gt;example&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt; Developers and AI agents can search, check availability, and register domains at cost directly from their editor, their terminal, or their agent â without leaving their workflow.&quot;],&quot;featured&quot;:[0,false],&quot;html&quot;:[0,&quot;&lt;p&gt;Today we&amp;#39;re launching the next chapter of Cloudflare Registrar: the &lt;b&gt;Registrar API in beta&lt;/b&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The Registrar API makes it possible to search for domains, check availability, and register them programmatically. Now, buying a domain the moment an idea starts to feel real no longer has to pull you out of the agentic workflow.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;A Registrar API has been one of the clearest asks from builders using Cloudflare. As more of the agentic workflow has moved into editors, terminals, and agent-driven tools, domain registration became the obvious gap to close.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;When we launched &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/products/registrar/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cloudflare Registrar&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; seven years ago, the idea was simple. Domains should be offered &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/application-services/solutions/low-cost-domain-names/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;at cost&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, with no markup and no games. Since then, Cloudflare Registrar has become one of the fastest growing registrars in the world as more people choose Cloudflare as the place to build their next project.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div style=\&quot;position: relative; padding-top: 53.93225331369662%;\&quot;&gt;\n  &lt;iframe\n    src=\&quot;https://customer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com/8e4b034c73d9e0a94c2070e9d1401bf6/iframe?muted=true&amp;preload=true&amp;loop=true&amp;autoplay=true&amp;poster=https%3A%2F%2Fcustomer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com%2F8e4b034c73d9e0a94c2070e9d1401bf6%2Fthumbnails%2Fthumbnail.jpg%3Ftime%3D%26height%3D600\&quot;\n    loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;\n    style=\&quot;border: none; position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; height: 100%; width: 100%;\&quot;\n    allow=\&quot;accelerometer; gyroscope; autoplay; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture;\&quot;\n    allowfullscreen=\&quot;true\&quot;\n  &gt;&lt;/iframe&gt;\n&lt;/div&gt;\n&lt;p&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;Prompting an agent inside an AI code editor to generate name ideas, search, check, and purchase a domain.&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;built-for-agents-and-automation\&quot;&gt;Built for agents and automation&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#built-for-agents-and-automation\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;The Registrar API is designed to work well anywhere software is already being built: inside editors, deployment pipelines, backend services, and agent-driven workflows.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The workflow is intentionally simple and machine-friendly. &lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;Search&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; returns candidate names. &lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;Check&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; returns real-time availability and pricing. &lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;Register&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; takes a minimal request and returns a workflow-shaped response that can complete immediately or be polled if it takes longer. That makes it straightforward to use for traditional API clients and for &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/learning/ai/what-is-agentic-ai/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;AI agents&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; acting on a user&amp;#39;s behalf.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;In practice, all this means that an agent can help with the full flow: suggest names, confirm which one is actually registrable, surface the price for approval, and then complete the purchase without forcing the user out of the tool they are already using.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;the-registrar-api\&quot;&gt;The Registrar API&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-registrar-api\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;At its core, this first release of the Registrar API does three things&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;Search&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; for domains&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;Check&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; availability&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;Register&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; domains&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;p&gt;For a curated set of popular TLDs to start, see the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/api/resources/registrar\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Registrar API docs&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. When supported, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/learning/dns/glossary/premium-domains/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;premium domains&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; can also be registered, but they require explicit fee acknowledgement.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The Registrar API is part of the full Cloudflare API, which means agents already have access to it today through the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/code-mode-mcp/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cloudflare MCP&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. It does not require a separate integration or a custom tool definition. An agent working in Cursor, Claude Code, or any MCP-compatible environment can discover and call Registrar endpoints using the same &lt;code&gt;search()&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code&gt;execute()&lt;/code&gt; pattern that covers the entire Cloudflare API surface. The moment the API was part of our spec, it was ready for agents.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;i&gt;What it looks like in practice&lt;/i&gt;:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;You&amp;#39;re building a new project in your favorite AI code editor. Halfway through scaffolding, you ask your agent: &amp;quot;Find me a good .dev domain for this project and register it.&amp;quot;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The agent searches for candidate names based on your project. It checks real-time availability for the one you pick and confirms the price. You say yes. It registers the domain, using your account&amp;#39;s default contact info and payment method automatically. By the time you&amp;#39;ve read the response, the domain is registered, and privacy is on.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Three API calls. A few seconds.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;i&gt;What it looks like in code&lt;/i&gt;:&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;step-1-search-for-domain-names\&quot;&gt;Step 1: &lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;Search&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; for domain names&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#step-1-search-for-domain-names\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Use the &lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;search&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; endpoint to submit a domain query, with or without a domain extension.&lt;/p&gt;\n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-JavaScript\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-JavaScript\&quot;&gt;async () =&amp;gt; {\n  return cloudflare.request({\n    method: &amp;quot;GET&amp;quot;,\n    path: `/accounts/${accountId}/registrar/domain-search`,\n    query: { q: &amp;quot;acme corp&amp;quot;, limit: 3 },\n  });\n}&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            \n            &lt;pre class=\&quot;language-JSON\&quot;&gt;&lt;code class=\&quot;language-JSON\&quot;&gt;{\n  &amp;quot;success&amp;quot;: true,\n  &amp;quot;errors&amp;quot;: [],\n  &amp;quot;messages&amp;quot;: [],\n  &amp;quot;result&amp;quot;: {\n    &amp;quot;domains&amp;quot;: [\n      {\n        &amp;quot;name&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;8.57&amp;quot;\n        }\n      },\n      {\n        &amp;quot;name&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;acmecorp.dev&amp;quot;,\n        &amp;quot;registrable&amp;quot;: true,\n        &amp;quot;tier&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;standard&amp;quot;,\n        &amp;quot;pricing&amp;quot;: {\n          &amp;quot;currency&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;USD&amp;quot;,\n          &amp;quot;registration_cost&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;10.11&amp;quot;,\n          &amp;quot;renewal_cost&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;10.11&amp;quot;\n        }\n      },\n      {\n        &amp;quot;name&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;acmecorp.app&amp;quot;,\n        &amp;quot;registrable&amp;quot;: true,\n        &amp;quot;tier&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;standard&amp;quot;,\n        &amp;quot;pricing&amp;quot;: {\n          &amp;quot;currency&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;USD&amp;quot;,\n          &amp;quot;registration_cost&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;11.00&amp;quot;,\n          &amp;quot;renewal_cost&amp;quot;: &amp;quot;11.00&amp;quot;\n        }\n      }\n    ]\n  }\n}&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/code&gt;\n            \n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;step-2-check-availability-and-pricing\&quot;&gt;Step 2: &lt;code&gt;&lt;b&gt;Check&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/code&gt; availability and pricing&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#step-2-check-availability-and-pricing\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot;
